export.CheckpointSet, numWorkers uint) ([]*metricpb.ResourceMetrics, error) {\n80: \trecords, errc := source(ctx, exportSelector, cps)\n81: \n82: \t// Start a fixed number of goroutines to transform records.\n83: \ttransformed := make(chan result)\n84: \tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n85: \twg.Add(int(numWorkers))\n86: \tfor i := uint(0); i < numWorkers; i++ {\n87: \t\tgo func() {\n88: \t\t\tdefer
